# UDU corpus, life event `career`

**2962 rules.** Career and profession. Trade named, job versus business, rank, employer, success, failure, transfers.

Feeds protocol E03 and E04. Grouped by nakshatra, then in corpus order. Each line carries its rule ID, its placement, its source book and page, and its polarity and confidence, in the form `polarity/confidence` where + is benefic, - is malefic, ~ is mixed.

- **AVA-2-1549** · nak Punarvasu, planet Mars · AVA-2 p178 · +/stated

  **THEN** The action pattern is simple and reliable: the first attempt fails, the second wins; career success comes through retrying rather than through force and placements that look weak in a young chart often deliver fully a decade later.
- **AVA-2-1552** · nak Punarvasu, planet Mercury · AVA-2 p178 · +/stated

- **AVA-2-1559** · nak Punarvasu, planet Rahu · AVA-2 p179 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Punarvasu

Read the fall as an interval rather than an outcome.
- **AVA-2-1566** · nak Punarvasu, house 3 · AVA-2 p181 · +/stated

Transits bring a project resumed, a sibling reconnected or a second effort that finally works.
- **AVA-2-1573** · nak Punarvasu, house 10 · AVA-2 p183 · +/stated
  **IF** Tenth bhava madhya in Punarvasu
  **THEN** Career is a sequence of restarts - the path breaks, changes and resumes and growth is anything but linear, yet the eventual position is stable and respected; teaching, counselling, writing, healing and advisory work suit best. The risk is mistaking a dip for an ending. Transits bring a career change, a restart or the stable rise that follows several attempts.

## Pushya — career (103)

- **ASH-0004** · nak Pushya · ASH p10 · +/stated

  **THEN** Success as church, religious or spiritual leader and in charitable works.
- **ASH-0006** · nak Pushya · ASH p10 · +/stated
  **IF** Pushya prominent
  **THEN** Excels in corporate or governmental occupations: law enforcement, armed forces, border protection, lawyers, courts, heads of state.
- **ASH-0013** · nak Pushya · ASH p11 · ~/stated

  **THEN** Moves into a position imposing established order through official means; works extreme hours at a job he dislikes, for family and society.
- **ASH-0016** · nak Pushya · ASH p11 · +/stated
  **IF** Pushya prominent
  **THEN** Excels in carpentry, construction and inspection work guaranteeing obedience to regional standards.
- **ASH-0017** · nak Pushya · ASH p11 · +/stated
  **IF** Pushya prominent
  **THEN** Participates in traditional events as musician, artist, planner, caterer or ceremonial/organisational representative.
- **ASH-0019** · nak Pushya, planet Saturn · ASH p12 · +/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Pushya
  **THEN** Work as regulator in ocean or maritime habitats, naturalist, shipbuilder and related fields governed by strict rules.
- **ASH-0673** · nak Pushya · ASH p197 · +/stated
  **IF** Pushya prominent
  **THEN** Traditional work, aristocrat, teacher, astrologer, priest, spiritual guru, education, psychotherapist, medical professional, creative work, nun, dairy, charity, politician, manager, counsellor, caterer, ruler, real estate, charitable organisation, food, painter, healer, clergy, psychologist, psychic, writer, hotelier, government officer, artisan.
- **ASH-0679** · nak Pushya · ASH p202 · +/stated
  **IF** Pushya prominent
  **THEN** Full of optimism, social respect, recognition, position and prosperity; good at heart, supportive, dutiful and honest, and conserves social values.
- **ASH-0682** · nak Pushya · ASH p202 · +/stated
  **IF** Pushya prominent (symbolised by the flower)
  **THEN** Shines in life and reaches a high position, spreading the fragrance of success and continuous growth, remaining in the upper section of society and living prosperously.
- **ASH-0684** · nak Pushya · ASH p202 · +/stated
  **IF** Pushya prominent
  **THEN** Focuses on the set rules of society, community, organisation, religion or caste and imposes punishment on those who break them; drawn to organisations where discipline and procedure are mandatory - defence, police, lawyers, security, judges.
- **ASH-0690** · nak Pushya · ASH p203 · +/stated
  **IF** Pushya female native
  **THEN** Self-sufficient with a good chance of succeeding in any field; most of her funds come from land and are spent on land resources, and she is best suited to a career in real estate.
- **ASH-0693** · nak Pushya, pada 1 · ASH p203 · +/stated
  **IF** Pushya pada 1
  **THEN** Leads a large political group or organisation as central administrative figure; respect and recognition from foreign lands, good understanding with father or boss, and name and fame through dedication to work.
- **ASH-0697** · nak Pushya, pada 2 · ASH p204 · +/stated
  **IF** Pushya pada 2
  **THEN** Prefers a job to business or enterprise; strong sense of servitude, supports subordinates, and attains high position in society or government office serving a large group selflessly; expert in scheduling and planning and a hard worker.
- **ASH-0700** · nak Pushya, pada 3 · ASH p204 · +/stated
  **IF** Pushya pada 3 (Libra navamsha, Venus-ruled)

- **ASH-0711** · nak Pushya, planet Sun · ASH p206 · ~/stated

  **THEN** Mars becomes a warrior of power and survival, applying energy strategically to protect; well placed this gives strong courage and success in police, military and enforcement roles, while afflicted it gives aggression, revenge and destructive action once the ego is engaged.
- **AVA-2-8605** · nak Jyeshtha, planet Mercury · AVA-2 p443 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mercury placed in Jyeshtha, its own nakshatra (karakatwa reading)
  **THEN** Mercury becomes sharp, strategic and psychologically aware, producing an excellent strategist with persuasive, commanding speech; afflicted it turns manipulative, deceitful and given to secret dealing bent to private advantage.
- **AVA-2-8608** · nak Jyeshtha, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p444 · ~/stated
  **IF** Saturn placed anywhere in Jyeshtha (karakatwa reading)
  **THEN** Saturn becomes burdened power and karmic pressure, giving high authority after struggle and the capacity to carry heavy responsibility when well placed, but loneliness, harshness and unethical earning when afflicted.
- **AVA-2-8613** · nak Jyeshtha, house 3 · AVA-2 p446 · ~/stated
  **IF** Third bhava madhya falling in Jyeshtha
  **THEN** Effort is bold and exposing - this native investigates, writes and argues with real nerve for information others avoid, while siblings bring the closest version of the whole star's recurring rivalry over property and precedence.
- **AVA-2-8620** · nak Jyeshtha, house 10 · AVA-2 p447 · ~/stated
  **IF** Tenth bhava madhya falling in Jyeshtha
  **THEN** Career is authority itself, suited to government, law, police and crisis work, rising after struggle with a professional image guarded and defended when threatened; the risks are politics, ego clashes and the corruption that available power quietly offers.
- **RBS-2-2043** · nak Jyeshtha · RBS-2 p514 · ~/stated
  **IF** Professions suited to Jyeshtha Nakshatra natives
  **THEN** All policing professions, government and administrative posts, oratory, firefighting, occultism (mainly black magic), detective work, organized crime, politics, bureaucracy, military service, elder-care work such as the Salvation Army, athletics especially sprinting, and managerial or entrepreneurial roles requiring military-style discipline, investigation and law-and-order skills.

  **THEN** Identity is built on integrity and authority is exercised as responsibility rather than privilege; leadership works through example and endurance, giving standing that is unchallengeable because it was earned publicly and slowly, strongest in the fourth quarter. The father is dignified and influential. Well placed: long life, authority, influence and genuine protection. Afflicted: rigidity, stubbornness, pride and the fall that follows when ambition outgrows ethics.
- **AVA-2-14256** · nak Uttara Ashadha, planet Mars · AVA-2 p511 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mars in Uttara Ashadha (karakatwa reading)
  **THEN** Mars becomes executive rather than impulsive - action planned, sustained and carried to completion; this is the officer's Mars, suited to defence, army, police, administration and command, with real physical discipline. Well placed: authority won through effort, a top position and courage that does not exhaust itself. Afflicted: harshness, criminal association, physical strain and disability and a wandering that never settles into achievement.
- **AVA-2-14260** · nak Uttara Ashadha, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p512 · ~/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Uttara Ashadha (karakatwa reading)
  **THEN** Saturn and this star agree completely: both build slowly, both demand responsibility before reward and both produce results that hold, with endurance exceptional and the authority that eventually arrives unassailable because every stage of it was earned. Well placed: administrative power, high standing reached late, metaphysical learning and durability that outlasts every rival. Afflicted: harshness, cruelty, a wandering and unsettled life, forfeited relationships and the tendency to take what has not been given.
- **AVA-2-14261** · nak Uttara Ashadha, planet Rahu · AVA-2 p513 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Uttara Ashadha (karakatwa reading)
  **THEN** Rahu amplifies the star's ambition into something formidable and occasionally unprincipled - the desire for status intensifies and this native rises through organisations and gathered influence rather than alone. Well placed: substantial worldly success, institutional power and gains through association and foreign links. Afflicted: ambition pursued past the dharma this star requires, boasting, the use of people for advancement and the characteristic Uttara Ashadha fall - a rise that collapses precisely because the ethics were treated as optional.
- **AVA-2-14267** · nak Uttara Ashadha, house 3 · AVA-2 p514 · +/stated
  **IF** Third bhava madhya in Uttara Ashadha
  **THEN** Effort here is methodical and unglamorous and it finishes - this native completes what others abandon and success is proportional to persistence rather than talent; communication is deliberate and carries weight, suiting teaching, writing and advisory work.
- **AVA-2-14277** · nak Uttara Ashadha, house 7 · AVA-2 p515 · +/stated
  **IF** Seventh bhava madhya in Uttara Ashadha
  **THEN** Business partnerships are stable and structured.
- **AVA-2-14282** · nak Uttara Ashadha, house 10 · AVA-2 p516 · +/stated
  **IF** Tenth bhava madhya in Uttara Ashadha
  **THEN** This is the star's own field and its strongest degree - professional life is built slowly and permanently in government, administration, law, teaching, industry or public service, with authority accumulating over decades and recognition arriving late and not withdrawn. The risk is the star's central warning: a rise pursued past ethics collapses precisely when the dharma is set aside.
- **AVA-2-20476** · nak Uttara Ashadha · AVA-2 p668 · +/stated

- **AVA-2-14413** · nak Shravana, house 10 · AVA-2 p531 · ~/stated

Supported: standing as a teacher, administrator, lawyer, judge, manager, consultant, media figure or institutional leader, respected by seniors. Afflicted: a career harmed through gossip, politics, hidden enemies or arrogance, and the reserve of the type misread as disinterest.

Afflicted: a socially awkward manner, intelligence misused to cause trouble and the loss of good company.
- **AVA-2-14418** · nak Shravana, planet Mars · AVA-2 p534 · ~/stated

Afflicted: wealth lost through disputes, a fault-finding harshness and a mental restlessness that exhausts the body.
- **AVA-2-14422** · nak Shravana, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p535 · ~/stated

Public and institutional work suits. Supported: administrative authority, discipline that outlasts rivals and standing earned slowly and kept. Afflicted: emotional coldness, heaviness of mind, difficulty concerning the mother and a life so dutiful that the receptivity beneath it is never expressed. Saturn holds a sensitive degree here.
- **AVA-2-14423** · nak Shravana, planet Rahu · AVA-2 p535 · ~/stated

Afflicted: manipulation through speech, rumour spread deliberately, scandal and reputation collapsing through exactly the channel that built it. - **AVA-2-14558** · nak Dhanishtha, house 3 · AVA-2 p552 · ~/stated

Supported: genuine artistic or athletic mastery and success in media. Afflicted: sibling rivalry, accidents through haste and effort spent chasing applause.
- **AVA-2-14565** · nak Dhanishtha, house 10 · AVA-2 p554 · ~/stated
  **IF** Tenth lord placed in Dhanishtha
  **THEN** Career is visible, rhythmic and ambitious - suited fields are music and performance, sport, defence, engineering, land and agriculture, government service, media and public leadership, and the state recognition this star is known for often arrives here; the rise is self-made and steady. Supported: fame, state recognition and authority in a public field. Afflicted: ambition outrunning ethics, a fall after visible success and reputation spent on display.
- **AVA-2-14568** · nak Dhanishtha, planet Sun · AVA-2 p556 · ~/stated

  **THEN** Authority becomes visible and rhythmic, sought through demonstrated achievement rather than inherited position; this native wants the limelight and generally earns it, rising from modest beginnings through courage and timing, with government and state recognition featuring strongly. The father is capable and often well placed. Supported: fame, public authority, honours from the state and leadership of groups. Afflicted: ego, harshness, a rise pursued unscrupulously and the fall that follows when the drum is mistaken for the drummer.
- **AVA-2-14570** · nak Dhanishtha, planet Mars · AVA-2 p556 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mars in Dhanishtha (karakatwa reading), rules Dhanishtha
  **THEN** Mars rules Dhanishtha and reaches full expression here, doubled in the fourth quarter where it also owns the navamsha; action is rhythmic and forceful - the drum is Mars's own instrument - giving athletic prowess, competitive success, surgical and technical capability and the nerve to lead in a crisis. Supported: sporting and martial excellence, leadership under pressure and protection of the weak. Afflicted: cruelty, harshness, family dispute, accidents and the marital discord this star is known for.
- **AVA-2-14571** · nak Dhanishtha, planet Mercury · AVA-2 p557 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mercury in Dhanishtha (karakatwa reading)
  **THEN** Mercury becomes the star's craftsman and the second quarter is its own navamsha and finest ground here - practical intelligence, fine coordination of mind and hand and real skill in music, performance and public relations, with sweet and carrying speech. Supported: mastery of a craft, stage capability and success in media and communication. Afflicted: cunning, cheating, fluctuating means and cycles of success and failure that demand a consistency this placement finds difficult.
- **AVA-2-14574** · nak Dhanishtha, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p557 · ~/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Dhanishtha (karakatwa reading)
  **THEN** Saturn supplies what this star most needs - the discipline that turns rhythm into mastery and abundance into permanence; its influence is present throughout the Capricorn half and its own placement doubles it, and the rise is slow, self-made and durable. Supported: craft mastered over decades, authority earned late and kept and wealth that holds because it was built. Afflicted: emotional heaviness, harshness, a life of unrelieved effort and the family friction that follows when duty leaves no room for warmth.
- **AVA-2-14575** · nak Dhanishtha, planet Rahu · AVA-2 p557 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Dhanishtha (karakatwa reading)
  **THEN** Rahu amplifies a star already built for recognition and the result is mass appeal - Dhanishtha's magnetism joined to Rahu's visibility, often worn with a controversial face; foreign gains and unconventional routes to fame both feature. Supported: genuine mass following, success in media and performance and rapid rise. Afflicted: dubious associations, herd influence, addiction, scandal and gains that spike and crash.
- **AVA-2-14580** · nak Dhanishtha, house 3 · AVA-2 p559 · +/stated

  **THEN** The star's own house - skill, rhythm, performance and communication all sit here; music, sport and media reward this degree and the abilities are built by repetition until they become instinct. Courage is physical and real and siblings are competitive and often talented in the same fields.
- **AVA-2-14588** · nak Dhanishtha, house 10 · AVA-2 p560 · ~/stated

The risk is the fall that follows when ambition outruns the ethics.

  **THEN** Austerity and endurance, with authority arriving late and holding - the period in which the tapasya becomes literal.
- **AVA-2-20480** · nak Purva Bhadrapada · AVA-2 p670 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purva Bhadrapada prominent
  **THEN** Suits death and transformation work, fire and high-temperature industries, mechanical and transport fields, and psychiatry or occult work, with ideological extremism and dark entertainment in afflicted forms.

## Uttarabhadrapada — career (78)

- **ASH-1912** · nak Uttarabhadrapada · ASH p486 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada prominent
  **THEN** Charity work, librarian, therapist, researcher, occultist, clerk, philosopher, historian, counsellor, yoga trainer, night watchman, meditation guru, hermit, astrologer, palmist.
- **ASH-1922** · nak Uttarabhadrapada · ASH p489 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada prominent
  **THEN** Emphasises abilities over qualifications - usually not well educated but eager to practise and master new skills, subsisting through the capacity to multitask; interested in the fine arts, a good writer, and most have articles and books published.
- **ASH-1923** · nak Uttarabhadrapada · ASH p489 · ~/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada native
  **THEN** Only has a solid career after he marries; the most likely paths are non-profit work, astrology, psychic work, yoga teaching and counselling.

  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada male native's career

- **ASH-1942** · nak Uttarabhadrapada, house 1 · ASH p494 · +/stated

- **AVA-2-17691** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, planet Mars · AVA-2 p622 · +/stated

- **AVA-2-17701** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, planet Rahu · AVA-2 p624 · +/stated

- **AVA-2-17730** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, planet Sun · AVA-2 p628 · +/stated
  **IF** Sun dasha, Sun placed in Uttara Bhadrapada

- **AVA-2-17732** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, planet Mars · AVA-2 p628 · ~/stated

  **IF** Saturn dasha, Saturn placed in Uttara Bhadrapada (own nakshatra)
  **THEN** The ruler's own period and the most Uttara Bhadrapada-flavoured stretch of the life - austerity, endurance and authority arriving late and holding permanently, with fourth-quarter Saturns taking their deepest tapasya here.
- **AVA-2-20481** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada · AVA-2 p670 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttara Bhadrapada prominent
  **THEN** Suits spiritual and therapeutic work, charity and renunciation, food and community work, death and medical work, and protective or hazardous fields, along with personal-care crafts and literature.

  **THEN** Can master several subjects at once and write extensively; not lazy, and once he undertakes a job he sees it through; is employed and reaches the top even from a poor family, with stability beginning after marriage and important career changes at ages 19, 21, 28, 30, 35 and 42.
- **RBS-2-1815** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada · RBS-2 p384 · +/stated
  **IF** Female born in Uttara Bhadrapada Nakshatra

- **RBS-2-1817** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada · RBS-2 p385 · +/stated

- **RBS-2-1822** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada · RBS-2 p386 · ~/stated

  **THEN** These are symbolic domains describing the kind of work a person is suited to and the quality he brings to it, not literal career predictions; the tenth house, its lord, the tenth from the Moon, the amatyakaraka and the running dasha remain the primary judges, with the nakshatra layer refining rather than replacing them.
- **AVA-2-20454** · AVA-2 p660 · ~/stated

- **RBS-2-2054** · planet Venus · RBS-2 p522 · +/stated
  **IF** Venus's influence on education or profession
  **THEN** Gives aptitude for music, dance, arts and painting at the liberal level, hotel management and tourism at the semi-technical level, and computer animation/graphics at the fully technical level.
- **RBS-2-2055** · planet Saturn · RBS-2 p522 · ~/stated
  **IF** Saturn's influence on education or profession
  **THEN** Gives aptitude for ancient learning, labour and law at the liberal level, mechanical work and geology at the semi-technical level, and engineering at the fully technical level.
- **RBS-2-2056** · planet Rahu · RBS-2 p522 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rahu's influence on education or profession

- **RBS-2-2057** · planet Ketu · RBS-2 p522 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ketu's influence on education or profession
  **THEN** Gives aptitude for language study at the liberal level, language-specialist work at the semi-technical level, and meteorology or computing at the fully technical level.
- **RBS-2-2058** · house 2 · RBS-2 p523 · ~/stated
  **IF** Assigning house roles for career analysis
  **THEN** Houses 1, 2, 10 and 11 govern profession/service generally; 2nd and 11th are analyzed for progress in profession, 10th for growth in position, 8th and 12th (with the 4th afflicted) for going abroad; the Ascendant shows the current job and boss, the 7th house the future job and boss, and the 12th house shows continuity in the current job and retirement.
- **RBS-2-2059** · planet Mercury, house 2 · RBS-2 p523 · +/stated
  **IF** A strong, unafflicted 2nd house, its lord and its karaka Mercury
  **THEN** Gives a sweet, musical voice with vocal and singing power, expressive facial features and dramatic skill suited to acting, along with good money and a good vehicle.
- **RBS-2-2060** · house 10 · RBS-2 p523 · ~/stated
  **IF** Assessing profession from the Dashamsha (D-10) divisional chart
  **THEN** The strength of the 10th lord in D-10 and the position and strength of the D-10 Lagna lord must be given equal weight in profession analysis, or the reading can be misleading.
- **RBS-2-2062** · planet Sun, house 10 · RBS-2 p523 · +/stated
  **IF** Sun placed in the 10th house
  **THEN** Money is earned through medicines, fragrance-related business, gold and wool; the native may be a doctor, medical officer, medical attendant or nurse, and also inherits paternal property.

  **THEN** Houses 7, 8 and 12 signified.

  **THEN** The prime career houses are 6 and 10; although the 12th-from-rule would make 5 and 9 negating houses, 5 and 9 are Trikona houses and never negate an event, so 8 and 12 are the true houses that negate or hurt career.
- **RBS-2-2076** · house 10 · RBS-2 p525 · +/stated

  **THEN** A native with more planets signifying the 10th and 11th houses reaches greater career heights and respect than one whose planets mainly signify the 2nd, 6th and 10th houses; the relative strength ranking is 2nd < 6th < 10th < 11th house, with the 11th house able to fulfil any desire; celebrities and billionaires typically have five or six planets signifying the 10th and 11th houses, while a brief sensation has only one or two.
- **RBS-2-2077** · planet Venus, house 10 · RBS-2 p525 · +/stated
  **IF** Venus strongly signifying houses 6, 10 and 11
  **THEN** Indicates the native becomes a top-level executive in a multinational corporation.

  **THEN** Indicates excessive opposition and stress.
- **RBS-2-2080** · house 6 · RBS-2 p526 · -/stated

  **THEN** Indicates failure in competition or theft of hard-earned money.
- **RBS-2-2082** · house 6 · RBS-2 p526 · -/stated
  **IF** A planet signifying houses 6, 8 and 12 together
  **THEN** Indicates stress, opposition, losses, troubles and suffering; among the three, house 6 is the least negative, followed by 8, then 12 (6 < 8 < 12 in severity).
- **RBS-2-2083** · house 6 · RBS-2 p526 · +/stated
  **IF** A planet signifying house 6 or 8 or 12 together with house 10 or 11
  **THEN** Signifying 6 with 11 brings a win in competition, 8 with 11 brings gain of unearned money, and 12 with 11 brings gain after expenditure; the 11th house lifts the level of success while the 10th house gives similar but lesser results.
- **RBS-2-2084** · RBS-2 p526 · ~/stated
  **IF** Reading ups and downs in career from the Mahadasha lord
  **THEN** When the Mahadasha lord signifies both good and bad career houses, the native experiences ups and downs in career; if the Mahadasha lord is strong and signifies houses 10 and 11, the career is stable overall even though a badly-placed Antardasha within it can still prevent the native from rising during that sub-period.
- **RBS-2-2085** · planet Sun, house 4 · RBS-2 p536 · ~/stated
  **IF** Combinations indicating a medical (doctor) profession
  **THEN** Houses or lords related to Sun, Saturn and Rahu, involving the 4th, 5th, 10th and 12th (hospitals and beds) houses, point to a medical career; an afflicted Moon inclines the native toward cruelty and administering drugs/poison (fitting surgery), while a strong Jupiter makes the native humane and benevolent as a healer.
- **RBS-2-2086** · house 5 · RBS-2 p536 · ~/stated
  **IF** Role of the 5th and 10th houses in medical aptitude
  **THEN** The 5th house denotes intelligence and, being 12th from the 6th, a strong 5th house or lord can negate the 6th house's disease-related significations; the 10th house denotes karma-kshetra and, being 5th from the 6th, denotes the skill of diagnosing disease, requiring a relation between the 5th and 10th houses and their connection to the 12th house, along with affliction of the Moon or 5th lord by malefics.
- **RBS-2-2088** · planet Sun · RBS-2 p537 · +/stated
  **IF** Planetary combinations for a medical/specialist-doctor profession
  **THEN** Sun and Jupiter are the principal karakas for healing; Sun with Jupiter indicates a physician, Sun with Mars indicates a surgeon, and Saturn's influence on a Sun-Jupiter association signifies homeopathy; a strong Rahu or Ketu associated with the 4th, 5th or 10th house alongside a well-placed Jupiter also favours the study of medicine.
- **RBS-2-2089** · house 1 · RBS-2 p537 · +/stated

  **THEN** Houses 6, 8 and 12 combined with 10 and 11; whichever of these houses is signified by more planets decides the type of cases handled — 6th house influence makes a divorce lawyer, 8th and 12th house influence makes a criminal lawyer.
- **RBS-2-2092** · house 2 · RBS-2 p541 · +/stated
  **IF** House combination for a teaching profession
  **THEN** Houses 2, 4, 5 and 9 combined with 10 and 11 for the full combination, which makes a university teacher with good earnings; a partial combination makes a school teacher, with income depending on how strongly the 11th house is signified.

  **THEN** Jupiter, Saturn, Uranus, Neptune and Pluto are the signifying planets, with Saturn as karaka of the 10th house of career; houses 2 (wealth), 9 (fate/prosperity), 10 (profession) and 11 (income) signify promotion, transfer or a rise in career, while houses 1, 8 and 12 negate it.

- **RBS-2-2140** · house 2 · RBS-2 p574 · ~/stated

  **THEN** Houses 2, 6, 10 and 11 together signify promotion, a rise in career or fortune, or reinstatement; houses 3, 10 and 12 signify a transfer with change of place (3rd for the journey/12th-from-4th absence from residence, 10th for employment, 12th for an unfamiliar setting); houses 1, 8, 9 and 12 signify suspension from service; houses 1, 5, 9 and 12 (plus house 8 if gratuity/provident fund applies) signify retirement, dismissal, or loss of status.

  **THEN** Produces Akhanda Samrajya Yoga; in its purest, unafflicted, strongly-vested form it gives the results of a very powerful Raja Yoga, while a version with only moderately strong yoga-causing planets instead makes the native wealthy, prosperous, high-ranking or politically powerful (illustratively strong for a Scorpio Lagna with Moon and Mars strong and Jupiter placed with them in the 10th house in Leo).

## Aridra — career (1)

- **RBS-2-2031** · nak Aridra · RBS-2 p508 · ~/stated
  **IF** Professions suited to Aridra Nakshatra natives
  **THEN** Mental-sport competitors (chess, scrabble, bridge), nuclear-power workers, roles involving deception (thieves, drug dealers, deceptive salespeople), electrical and electronic engineering, computer and software development, sound engineering, electronic musicians, English-language experts, weapons experts, film special-effects and computer-game design, virtual-reality experts, manual labourers, physicists, mathematicians and researchers, philosophers, science-fiction writers, and surgeons or doctors who administer small doses of poison as remedies (homeopaths, allopaths).

## Ashwini — career (2)

- **AVA-2-20456** · nak Ashwini · AVA-2 p661 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashwini prominent (tenth lord, tenth bhava madhya or strongest planet)
  **THEN** Suits vehicles and mobility, sport and action-oriented work, medicine and healing, engineering and heavy industry, nature-related fields, construction and foundational work, and public or commercial fields including law, advertising and the armed forces.
- **RBS-2-2026** · nak Ashwini · RBS-2 p505 · +/stated
  **IF** Professions suited to Ashwini Nakshatra natives
  **THEN** All healing professions (surgeons, physicians, counsellors, physiotherapists, childbirth specialists), mechanical and general engineering, horse trainers and racing enthusiasts, gamblers, athletes and sport-related jobs, motor sports and adventure sports, explorers, stunt men, researchers and pioneers, soldiers, generals, and jewellers especially goldsmiths.

## Purva Phalguni — career (1)

- **AVA-2-20466** · nak Purva Phalguni · AVA-2 p665 · +/stated
  **IF** Purva Phalguni prominent
  **THEN** Suits entertainment and arts, beauty and fashion, events and relationships, education and management, and food and design professions.

## Uttara Phalguni — career (1)

- **AVA-2-20467** · nak Uttara Phalguni · AVA-2 p665 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttara Phalguni prominent
